wet_prepare_border_war_A01 = {
 allowed = { is_ai = yes }
 enable = {
  wet_ai_ready_for_border_war = yes
  has_country_flag = wet_ai_war_pending
  is_in_array = { wet_ai_war_targets = A01 }
  A01 = { wet_ai_border_target = yes }
 }
 abort_when_not_enabled = yes
 ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = A01 value = 200 }
}
wet_order_friendship_A01 = {
 allowed = { is_ai = yes }
 enable = {
  has_government = democratic
  NOT = { tag = A01 }
  NOT = { has_war_with = A01 }
  A01 = { exists = yes has_government = democratic }
 }
 abort_when_not_enabled = yes
 ai_strategy = { type = befriend id = A01 value = 200 }
 ai_strategy = { type = alliance id = A01 value = 150 }
}
